# AURA — Complete One-File Antigravity Handoff

> **Project:** AURA — Agent Uncertainty and Residue Arbiter  
> **Repository:** <https://github.com/shanmukhdatta/AURA>  
> **Handoff date:** 25 October 2026  
> **Current research position:** Stage 8.2 verified controlled external-SQL pilot; next Stage 8.3 independent agent evaluation.  
> **Delivery:** ONE Markdown file that contains the instructions **and** a checksum-verified compressed recovery copy of historical source, tests, and experiment evidence.  
> **Operating mode:** Autonomous, evidence-first, stop/go checkpoints, zero invented results, and feature-branch pull request only.

## Your mission, Antigravity

You are an autonomous AI software engineer, experiment operator and skeptical research auditor. **Execute** the remaining AURA study, rather than merely summarizing a plan. Clone the repository, restore the full prior source from the embedded recovery archive in this file, rerun old tests, implement Stage 8.3, execute feasible genuine experiments, evaluate AURA's independent PASS/FAIL/UNCERTAIN certificates, compare to fair baselines, save evidence, and return a reproducible report for the human to paste into ChatGPT for independent review.

You must distinguish **what is already complete**, **what you completed during this run**, **what is partially complete**, **what was blocked**, and **what you only propose**. Do not fabricate an extra Stage 9 historical plan: find the original stage map in the repository or supplied documents, and preserve it. If no definitive Stage 9 specification exists, mark its boundaries *not verified*, then propose a Stage 9 reporting/replication plan clearly as a proposal, not as pre-agreed history.

**IMPORTANT: This is a single self-contained file.** The last section includes a BASE64-encoded ZIP with 136 files from our prior verified research. Read the instructions near the top; **do not paste the huge data appendix into an LLM chat prompt**. Use terminal/Python to recover it and inspect the files. If this `.md` appears only in the agent message and not on the filesystem, first save/upload this same file into the workspace, then perform the extraction. **Do not claim you cloned the entire implementation from GitHub alone**: GitHub currently contains project docs, but not the whole historical Stage 7 source tree, so recovery is essential.

## 0. Non-negotiable scientific and repository rules

1. Never invent agent outputs, benchmark runs, SQL observations, certificates, source commits, scores, timings, or test receipts.
2. Genuine LLM-generated tool calls, published benchmark oracle actions, scripted fault injections, mock integration tests and official benchmark scores are **different cohorts**. Record the cohort label for every run. Never combine denominators as if they were independent samples.
3. AURA's predictions must be saved and hashed **before** independent grader labels are revealed. Neither AURA nor its probe-selection policy may receive the grader's expected answers as input.
4. A tool saying `success` is not trusted proof. State must be read independently, with missing evidence resulting in `UNCERTAIN` if no actual observed violation establishes FAIL. Temporal requirements require real trusted ordered traces; final state alone cannot prove them.
5. Retain the previous source/results unchanged for auditing. Every code correction needs a reason, a regression test and a diff. **Do not doctor older results** to pass a new check.
6. No paid API, service subscription, public benchmark submission, third-party model training, forced push, history rewrite, or branch merge without explicit human authorization. Local/free inference or a Kaggle GPU notebook is acceptable if resources are available.
7. Keep secrets/private data/model weights/private hidden evaluators out of commits, PRs and shared artifacts. Audit staged changes (`git diff --cached`) for secrets and oversized files.
8. Prefer a new branch `experiment/stage-8-3-external-llm-verification` and a pull request to `main`. You have permission to create commits and push this feature branch when safe; **never push to main or merge the PR** without human approval.
9. If a gate is blocked, save the first relevant exception, environment/versions, exactly what did run, what remains unverified, and the smallest feasible correction. **Do not fake a later gate.**
10. Do not wait passively for all stages before producing results. Persist a `STATUS.md` and output receipts after every gate. If your environment offers long-running/autonomous sessions, use its normal checkpointing and user-visible progress tools rather than promising work outside available capabilities.

## 1. Known results and limitations (do not relabel)

| Work | What was actually executed | Scientific limit |
|---|---|---|
| AURA core Stages 0–7 | Prototype contracts, independent snapshot and evidence collectors, replayable PASS/FAIL/UNCERTAIN certificate engine, CERA planning, 186 regression tests passed on fresh rerun | Prototype, not production verification and not proof that CERA is always faster |
| Stage 8.1 local LLM | Four Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct local SQL/file agent workflows; AURA verdicts agreed with independent local reference on all four | Four designed test cases, not a general performance estimate |
| Stage 8.1 AppWorld | Three genuine AppWorld development tasks evaluated; Qwen native successes 0/3; 12 execution attempts | No AURA external AppWorld certificates produced; poor agent performance mostly invalid API actions |
| Stage 8.2 external SQL | Six externally authored AgentSynth `core_v2` task definitions executed in real SQLite with six SCRIPTED control variants per task, 36 certificates, 6 PASS/24 FAIL/6 UNCERTAIN, matching designed fault expectations | NOT 36 naturally occurring real-agent executions; native-style SQL checker reproduction, NOT official AgentSynth CLI |
| Stage 8.2 evidence ablations | 144 certificate recomputations across 36 shared states with different evidence-access masks; three tampering tests rejected | NOT 144 new independent tasks; Stage 8.2 did not measure CERA's actual cost advantage |

### Real findings worth preserving

- In **six controlled unauthorized-extra-edit episodes**, published-style required SQL outcome checks still passed, while AURA's **stronger locally declared side-effect contract** detected the persistent extra update and returned FAIL. This compares *different policy scopes*; it is not a fair superiority claim against an equivalently configured verifier.
- With essential independent inspections withheld in six designed cases, AURA abstained with `UNCERTAIN`. Do not re-label these simulated denials as real infrastructure failures.
- Previous CERA cost advantages were synthetic/assumption-dependent; tiny local real timings may be worse than greedy once planning overhead is included. Treat cost comparison as an open question.

### Upstream AgentSynth provenance

Repository: <https://github.com/agentsynth/agentsynth>  
Prior source paths: `packs/core_v2.yaml` (Git blob `d2bee0100fc46c58bbbe73b94d921b3e6c9f7a7a`) and `packs/core_v2_oracle.py` (Git blob `942c296fadd47c7ec335355af39de05db9f7024a`). Those are **Git blob SHA1 references**, not full pinned repository commit IDs. Pin the **actual upstream full commit SHA** during Stage 8.3. Inspect and retain the repository license notices when using upstream materials.

## 2. First terminal action — clone, restore and audit the entire available project

These steps are mandatory because the GitHub repository currently has project documents but lacks the historical implementation directory `AURA_Stage7/`.

1. Open a fresh workspace and save this Markdown file as `AURA_ANTIGRAVITY_COMPLETE_HANDOFF_ONE_FILE.md`. You may use `git clone https://github.com/shanmukhdatta/AURA.git AURA`, then `cd AURA`. If the agent cannot access GitHub due to authentication, write a precise blocker and reconstruct into a local working directory from the embedded archive without falsely claiming remote clone success.
2. Record `git remote -v`, `git status --short`, `git log -5 --oneline`, current HEAD SHA, and which tracked files exist. **Read** `README.md`, `research/PROJECT_STATUS_AND_GATES.md`, and `research/STAGE_8_3_ANTIGRAVITY_PROMPT.md` if present.
3. Create the feature branch. If it already exists, inspect the upstream/head and use a unique name or safely resume, without deleting work.
4. Run the following Python recovery script from your current working folder. Adapt only `MD_PATH` if your handoff Markdown is stored elsewhere. This script validates the embedded payload's SHA256, ZIP CRC and path safety and unpacks into `_aura_recovery/`; it does NOT overwrite the checkout.

```python
from pathlib import Path
from io import BytesIO
import base64, hashlib, zipfile

MD_PATH = Path("AURA_ANTIGRAVITY_COMPLETE_HANDOFF_ONE_FILE.md")
if not MD_PATH.is_file():
    # For example, when you cloned the repo but the user uploaded the MD elsewhere:
    raise FileNotFoundError("Copy the handed-off MD into this folder, then rerun")
raw = MD_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
start_marker = "<!-- AURA_" + "EMBEDDED_ZIP_BASE64_BEGIN -->"
end_marker   = "<!-- AURA_" + "EMBEDDED_ZIP_BASE64_END -->"
assert raw.count(start_marker) == 1 and raw.count(end_marker) == 1
encoded = "".join(raw.split(start_marker, 1)[1].split(end_marker, 1)[0].split())
archive = base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)
expected = "784fd8571dae33e138bb7b17c6d4a3f7366c1bc52239e6e2e79008d42f4eb95b"
actual = hashlib.sha256(archive).hexdigest()
assert actual == expected, f"Embedded payload SHA256 mismatch: {actual}"
root = Path("_aura_recovery")
root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
with zipfile.ZipFile(BytesIO(archive)) as z:
    assert z.testzip() is None, "Recovery ZIP CRC failure"
    for i in z.infolist():
        rel = Path(i.filename)
        assert not rel.is_absolute() and ".." not in rel.parts and not i.filename.startswith("/")
        target = root / rel
        if i.is_dir():
            target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        else:
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_bytes(z.read(i))
print("RECOVERY_OK", len(list(root.rglob("*"))), "filesystem entries")
print("Recovered source/test tree:", root / "AURA_Stage7")
print("Recovered executed experiment:", root / "stage82_agentsynth_pilot.py")
print("Recovered original evidence:", root / "AURA_Stage8_2_External_SQL_Evidence_v1.zip")
```

5. Verify `_aura_recovery/HANDOFF_MANIFEST_SHA256.json` by recomputing each recovered file hash/byte length and rejecting any mismatch. The embedded archive already has 136 archive members; verify that count rather than trusting this document. Verify nested Stage 8.2 evidence ZIP CRC and its `FILE_MANIFEST_SHA256.json` too.
6. **Do not automatically copy all recovery contents into GitHub.** Inspect duplicates and provenance. Put historical Stage 7 source/tests and Stage 8.2 scripts under their original paths in the working tree, keeping `research/` docs from the current remote branch intact. Keep immutable historical evidence under `artifacts/historical/` or external research storage, with digest receipts and links; exclude unreviewed/raw sensitive results from Git. Use `git status`/diff to confirm no accidental replacement of already tracked files. Remove `_aura_recovery/` from the Git staging area (or add to `.gitignore`) after importing only reviewed material.
7. Record a file-by-file import manifest (`stage83/results/historical_import_manifest.json`): source path, destination path, SHA256, preserved vs modified, copyright/license status, and exact source of truth. If the original Stage 0–6 code is not independently present, do not invent it; Stage 7 is the available complete implementation snapshot for this handoff.

## 4. Stage 8.3 execution gates — run sequentially

### G0. Baseline audit (CPU, mandatory)

- Re-run the **actual** inherited AURA tests, whose prior verified reference was 186 passing tests. If counts differ, explain why with exact logs. Save test stdout, stderr, wall time, versions, exit code, source/tree SHA256.
- Rerun Stage 8.2 from clean directory, inspect its 36 controlled episodes and 144 dependent ablation judgments; verify individual AURA certificates replay and that the output ZIP matches its own file hashes.
- Compare regenerated results with the immutable historical evidence by scope/metadata—not byte-identical timestamps if nondeterministic.
- Examine whether Stage 7 source and Stage 8.2 scripts contain unvalidated assumptions, especially coverage completeness, frame allowlists, deny-readback implementation, sensitive hidden read channels and any tool-result trust.
- Produce `stage83/results/g0_baseline_audit.json` and `stage83/G0_STATUS.md`. **G0 PASS** requires all checks executed and discrepancies itemized; if benchmark data cannot be accessed yet that is not a G0 failure.

### G1. Official AgentSynth harness (CPU, mandatory)

- Clone <https://github.com/agentsynth/agentsynth> and pin a full Git commit SHA; save license and upstream task pack file hashes. Pin a clean Python environment and confirm CLI syntax from `agentsynth --help`, not memory.
- Parse the **original 14-task** `core_v2` YAML. Determine which tasks are SQL-state only, tool-called, answer/refusal, multi-table and multirow. Mark unsupported contracts instead of silently scoring them.
- Run the benchmark's **official CLI/native scorer** on its published oracle (if accepted) and a no-op baseline using official tool interfaces; store raw results and first exceptions. Do not substitute our hand-coded replicated SQL predicates or assume the oracle trivially passes all checks.
- Freeze task selection, contract obligations and allowed-write scopes **before** real agent results are inspected; record independent reviewer/author (human or agent), reasons and SHA256. For ambiguous task instructions choose a conservative `UNCERTAIN`-capable contract and document the ambiguity.
- Produce `stage83/results/g1_official_benchmark.json` and `stage83/G1_STATUS.md` with exact official native scores and any task exclusions. **G1 PASS** requires an actual official scorer run; do not fake that with fixtures.

### G2. External-to-AURA adapter (CPU, mandatory)

- Use the existing real AURA Stage 7 verifier/certificate engine, not a newly implemented substitute. Inspect `AURA_Stage7/aura_external/`, `aura_evidence/`, `aura_verification/` and actual interfaces.
- Implement an AgentSynth runtime adapter with independently captured before/after SQLite snapshots, schema, ordered tool traces (actual SQL strings and parameters, invocation/result/error), task epochs, complete coverage receipts, and explicitly allowed effects.
- Take read-only snapshots and verify inspection noninterference by hashing original world database before/after the AURA evaluation. Account for WAL/journaling and avoid treating snapshot copy files as unauthorized agent side effects.
- Verify both required postconditions and **absence of persistent unauthorized modifications**, but do not claim to detect transient edits later undone unless trustworthy ordered mutation traces are available.
- Independently check answers and mandatory tool/process steps only when the trusted references cover them; missing channels must not be interpreted as evidence of success.
- Create actual replayable AURA certificate(s) for official upstream task states, with isolated reference results withheld until after verdicts are persisted.
- Test known PASS, known FAIL, denied readback -> UNCERTAIN when no violation otherwise proven, false tool success, forged evidence digest, tampered coverage and modified snapshot, and noninterference. Save test receipts.
- Produce `stage83/results/g2_adapter_audit.json` and `stage83/G2_STATUS.md`. **G2 PASS** requires actual independent external-world observation, replayable certificate and missing-evidence behavior, not mocked official evaluator JSON alone.

### G3. Genuine LLM-executed external tasks (GPU only if needed)

- Start with a preregistered **five-task** smoke sample from at least two task difficulty tiers, with clearly recorded task IDs and selection rules. Use actual model inference; suggested open-weight baseline `Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct` if available, or a documented free/local model. Record exact model revision, provider, prompts, tokenizer, generation settings, seeds, hardware, limits, wall time.
- Give model only official task instructions plus correct tool API/schema/docs and its own visible tool outputs. Do not leak official solution, oracle, hidden evaluation, expected outcome, or AURA verification verdict. Validate API schemas before real inference so we do not repeat AppWorld's bad-documentation issue.
- Execute actual model-generated SQL tool calls against official AgentSynth environment; log every proposed, accepted/rejected, executed, tool response and database state. Save any user-facing agent answer. No silent switch to scripted oracle when model fails.
- Immediately freeze and hash agent trajectories and AURA certificates **before** loading or inspecting official score/reference results.
- If five-task smoke is successful in terms of *engineering integrity* (not necessarily task success), expand to 14 tasks x 2–3 preregistered seeds as compute permits. Do not cherry-pick successful trials. Model task failures are valuable.
- Keep scripted fault variants in separate named cohorts. If only 5 tasks complete, publish honest pilot counts and don't label 14 executed.
- Produce `stage83/results/g3_real_agent_run_manifest.json` and `stage83/G3_STATUS.md`.

### G4. Fair analysis and CERA empirical comparison

- After certificates are hashed, open reference/native evaluator outcomes, and calculate exact task-level comparisons.
- The official native checker success label and AURA **strict contract** compliance are different outcomes. Implement a **matched-contract full-snapshot baseline** with identical allowed writes and event obligations, and disclose mismatches rather than claiming AURA dominance due to stronger policy.
- For each episode report `task_id, task_family, source_commit, model_id, seed, execution_mode, official_score, strict_reference, aura_verdict, uncertain_reasons, evidence_coverage, false_PASS, false_FAIL, probes, bytes_read, probe_cost_ms, planner_ms, verification_ms, wall_time, trace_provenance`.
- Compute denominators and eligible evaluation populations correctly; give false PASS/FAIL rates, decisive coverage, uncertainty rate, task-family stratification, event/process compliance, and side-effect violations. Bootstrap by task if sample size supports defensible intervals; label tiny samples exploratory.
- CERA is *not automatically better*: compare CERA against full, fixed, greedy and exhaustive policies when probe sets/costs/coverage are identical, and **include planning overhead**. If baselines don't obey same access contract, mark the cost comparison invalid rather than reporting an advantage.
- Consider inspection ablations and future AgentProcessBench (step label) data, but do not merge step-label accuracy into final-state success accuracy.
- Produce `stage83/results/g4_metrics.csv`, `stage83/results/g4_cost.csv`, `stage83/results/g4_mismatch_analysis.md`, `stage83/G4_STATUS.md`.

### G5. Reproducibility, GitHub feature-branch PR and final hand-back

- Create `stage83/README.md`, pinned dependencies/lockfiles, evaluation scripts, scripts for clean reruns, original tool interfaces/contract definitions, provenance docs, detailed negative-case analysis, and test receipts.
- Export immutable `AURA_STAGE8_3_RESULTS.zip` with all *permitted* experimental raw evidence: actual model trajectories, actual traces and SQL operations, actual before/after world states, probe observations, original certificates, independent reference/official scores, audit receipts, configuration and `SHA256SUMS.json`. Include a ZIP CRC and manifest recheck report. **Never put hidden official evaluator secrets or credential-bearing logs into public ZIP.**
- Do a security/data-license review before Git operations. Commit source/docs/test fixtures and small sanitized reproducible examples on `experiment/stage-8-3-external-llm-verification`, push the feature branch, and open a PR (no force push, no merge) when permitted. If GitHub access fails, save a patch and commands for manual PR; no invented URL.
- Explain all uncompleted gates and remaining research limitations and propose specific Stage 9 follow-up only based on earned evidence.
- Produce `stage83/G5_STATUS.md` and `AURA_STAGE83_HANDOFF_REPORT.md` with the exact machine-readable results below. The agent's final message must repeat the main conclusions in a human-readable way and point to real files/PR links.

## 6. Success criteria and scientific stop rules

- **Stage 8.2 remains unchanged**; reproducible historical evidence is a prerequisite, not the new contribution.
- **Minimum Stage 8.3 independent validation** = official upstream benchmark result(s) + genuine LLM-generated external tool actions + independent external SQLite evidence + actual AURA certificate(s) + held-out reference comparison + replay/negative tests.
- **Strong Stage 8.3 evidence** = five or more real LLM episodes spanning independent tasks, ideally 14 x multiple seeds, with precommitted contracts and fair matched-policy comparison.
- **Empirical CERA speed advantage** can only be claimed from comparable measured costs that include planning overhead; negative results must be retained.
- A claimed `PASS` requires complete relevant positive-obligation and frame evidence. Missing observations should yield `UNCERTAIN` except if some fully observed obligation already establishes a definite FAIL.
- No confidence interval or "real-world generality" from 36 designed controls or only 3 AppWorld dev tasks.

Model should see documented SQL-query tool schema, not hidden evaluators or oracle plans. Never silently label a scripted substitute as an LLM output. Save `AURA_STAGE83_REAL_AGENT_OUTPUTS.zip` and give exact download instructions. If the notebook cannot run on available Kaggle resources, report why and do not claim G3.
